## Journal objectives and implementation anchors

Objective labels come from the saved local journal export. They identify intent; the implementation transitions below establish graph evidence.

| Objective | Journal path |
| --- | --- |
| So you decided to ditch the City of Dreams – start anew, start fresh. I went through a few fresh starts in my life, too. And you know what? Don't regret a damn thing. You won't either. Give my regards to the South. And when you hear Johnny's voice, go ahead and smile, but don't bother to answer. It'll just be a voice on the radio recorded long ago, a fading reproduction, a flickering memory. You'll be someone completely different. Take care, V. | --- | --- | --- |
| `actor_readiness` | q202_01_one_for_the_road.questphase / h0 / 38; q202_01_one_for_the_road.questphase / h0 / 5; q202_04a_badlands_travel.scene / scene / 1004; q202_05_convoy.scene / scene / 8163; +3 locations in catalogue.json | community/entry or actor reference + count policy → actor available. Test already-spawned, delayed and missing actors before speech or commands. |
| `actor_route` | q202_01_motel.scene / scene / 2478; q202_05_convoy.scene / scene / 1433; q202_05_convoy.scene / scene / 1622; q202_05_convoy.scene / scene / 1761; +10 locations in catalogue.json | actor + authored route/spline + movement policy → movement output. World navigation is a dependency; test blockage, combat and departure recovery. |
| `call_lifecycle` | q202_06_border_running.scene / scene / 2649; q202_06_border_running.scene / scene / 2652 | contact + call operation + scene ownership → connection/end state. Audio filtering is not call connection; test busy, interrupted and ended calls. |

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

1071 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 150 distinct direct resource identities; 1 embedded graph handoffs. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.
